Turn 5 · 2 players moved 🏅 @rmkmau.bsky.social +3 · @direprism.bsky.social +3 🔔 Tags take you to the new board · "no tags" stops them 31 cells left, 3 spares, 12 flagged. Reply a coordinate, A1 to K11. Everyone who replies opens one. 30 min. Minesweeper 16bit playalong mathematics atproto



<div class="bsky-image-grid bsky-grid-1" markdown="1">



![Minesweeper, turn 5. 11 by 11 grid, rows A to K, columns 1 to 11. 24 mines. 66 of 97 safe cells opened, 12 flagged. Reading each row left to right: a digit is how many mines touch that cell, 0 is an opened blank, ? is still hidden, F is a cell somebody has flagged as a mine. A: 0 0 0 0 1 F F 3 F ? ? B: 0 0 0 0 1 2 2 3 F ? ? C: 0 0 1 1 1 0 0 1 2 ? ? D: 0 0 1 F 2 1 2 1 2 ? ? E: 0 0 1 1 2 F 3 F 4 ? ? F: 0 1 1 ? 1 1 3 F 4 F ? G: 1 2 F ? 1 0 1 1 3 ? ? H: ? ? 3 F 1 0 0 0 1 ? ? I: ? ? ? 3 2 1 2 2 2 1 1 J: ? ? ? ? ? ? ? F ? ? ? K: ? ? ? ? ? ? ? ? ? ? ? Last move I11 was safe and touches 1 mine.](https://cdn.bsky.app/img/feed_fullsize/plain/did:plc:uvaanponi7dhxeu3b4xbckev/bafkreiga2dyohlbhx5nvgjiavc3sy2fnlk4ig3n77r3bi5um3failpdyn4){ .bsky-img }

<div class="bsky-card" markdown="1">

<div class="bsky-header" markdown="1">

<img src="https://cdn.bsky.app/img/avatar/plain/did:plc:c22amt5vl4rw6ah6w64rejoc/bafkreidonftkeah7avo42kligud3c4a3miji6c573kfip5tdctr3vkpb44" class="bsky-avatar">

<br><small>2026-11-23</small>
</div>
</div>

<div class="bsky-content" markdown="1">

Bluesky handle on GitHub Pages won't verify by file? The default build skips dot-folders, so .well-known/atproto-did is a 404. Fix: put include: [".well-known"] in _config.yml. Working ones I checked all do. .nojekyll works too (tested), but only on plain HTML: it turns Jekyll themes off. atdev







</div>

With the schedule having to shift due to another booked event on Monday and a tight schedule, we skipped on the home-cooked curry and intend to prepare it at a future meetup. We also missed recording or streaming the event and would welcome some help from the community with setting a stream up for future events. While we only had 2 presentations this time, there was a good level of discussion on the topics ...</p>
<small>:material-link: https://otter.pckt.blog/community-in-otter-cybe...</small>

Blueskyが協賛した学生ハッカソンJPHACKS 2026の最終選考が先日行われ、Award Day Bluesky賞はTeam JISAKU @jisaku.dev さんを選ばせていただきました。🥇 作品は「災害用の通信網」で、多くのアプリに組み込まれることで圏外でも端末同士で情報をリレーできるという仕組みです。 ひとつの企業やアプリに依存せず、分散型のネットワークこそが強靭になりうるというコンセプトに共感しました。また、世界規模での標準化を実現するために社会実装を進めていこうと本気で取り組んでいる姿が印象的でした！ JPHACKS atproto
